1,332 entries, handover to heal. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **handover**: Dump the in-head topics of this session, triage each into a bucket (handover / memory / project-docs / do-now), act on them, and write a handover file for the next session to pick up. Leading keywords, any order — "ok" runs non-interactively with default buckets, "push" commits and pushes at the very end, "light" writes no handover file; any further text is a note to the next session ("/handover ok push resume once the env is prepared"). ([FlorinI/Florians-Claude-Code-Kit](https://github.com/FlorinI/Florians-Claude-Code-Kit/blob/HEAD/home/commands/handover.md) · ★5)
- **handover**: Compose and post a structured engineering handover to Slack — hand a body of work (PRs, Linear issues, an incident, a root cause) to a specific teammate for their review/decision. Accepts a target person and optional "draft" / channel as $ARGUMENTS. Mirrors the house Slack style of /daily-standup-post-slack. ([chimeranext/better-toolkits](https://github.com/chimeranext/better-toolkits/blob/HEAD/toolkits/make-no-mistakes-toolkit/commands/handover.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **harden**: Use after an implementation is finished — typically right after /feature-dev:tdd — to review it in fresh context (defects, spec conformance and tech debt at once), verify every finding, fix the ones you approve, and re-review what the fixes changed. - **HARNESS**: This harness provides a standard operating procedure (SOP) and toolkit for coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, etc.) to build powerful, stateful CLI interfaces for open-source GUI applications. The goal: let AI agents operate software that was designed for humans, without needing a display or mouse. - **hatch.de**: Wie SCHRAFF (HATCH, Alias SCH, GS) in AutoCAD eine Fläche schraffiert, Schritt für Schritt: Punkt oder Objekte, Muster, Maßstab, Winkel, Inseln. Zum Testen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.de.md))

- **hatch.ja**: AutoCAD のハッチング(HATCH、H)で領域を埋める方法をプロンプトの順に説明します。内側の点またはオブジェクト、パターン、尺度と角度、島。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ja.md))
- **hatch.ko**: AutoCAD 해치(HATCH, H), 캐드 해치를 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 내부 점 또는 객체 선택, 해치 패턴, 축척과 각도, 고립영역. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ko.md))
- **hatch.pl**: Jak kreskować w AutoCADzie poleceniem KRESKUJ (HATCH), monit po monicie: punkt wewnątrz lub obiekty obwiedni, wzór, skala, kąt, wyspy. Wypróbuj za darmo online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.pl.md))
- **hatch.pt-BR**: Como funciona o HATCH do AutoCAD em português, HACHURA (alias HC): ponto interno ou objetos, padrão, escala, ângulo e ilhas. Experimente grátis online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.pt-BR.md))
- **hatch.ru**: Как штриховать в AutoCAD командой ШТРИХ (HATCH), запрос за запросом: точка внутри или объекты контура, образец, масштаб, угол, островки. Попробуйте бесплатно. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ru.md))
